## 2026-08-29 — Consistency review #56: semantic batch 13 (104 workflows, 1,305 → 1,409 of 5,363 read; 26.3% coverage) — 75 defect spots repaired across 8 classes incl. the "ERB" phantom regulator (25 spots, 2 title cascades), legacy BIR 1601-E forms (×9), DO 174 taxonomy mis-attribution (×3), and a NEW corpus-wide class discovery: 176 missing-quantifier Volume/Frequency fields censused for a dedicated pass

**Batch 13** (104 workflows, seed 5757, 13-per-family expansion-weighted, excluding all 1,305 prior W-ids; 80 distinct VS, 57 VS-53+, 95 PA files), pre-screened (zero flags) then read in full.

- **"ERB" phantom regulator removed with two title cascades (25 spots):** VS-163 attributed EV-charging electrical inspection and utility/regulatory engagement to "ERB (Energy Regulatory Board)" — a body that has not existed since EPIRA (RA 9136, 2001) renamed it the ERC, and which never had EVSE certification anyway. Web-verified: the EV-charging regime is **DOE EVCS-provider accreditation under RA 11697** plus LGU/electrical (Philippine Electrical Code) inspection, with the **ERC** the correct regulator for net-metering/selling-energy interfaces. Repaired across W4774 (title + 6 body spots), W4785-adjacent PA-163.2 (×5), W4791 (title + 5 body spots), VS-163 README, CTL-140 in the internal-controls matrix, the W4774/W4791 criticality-register rows, and the gap-analysis (titles cascaded through header + TOC anchor + register; `fix-toc-anchors.py` rerun clean).
- **Legacy BIR form 1601-E → 1601-EQ (×9 spots, 4 files):** W293's ERP Tax Master mapping and a 5-spot straggler cluster in PA-17.3 (incl. the Frequency line, where 1601-EQ also moves from Monthly to Quarterly, and the monthly-forms arithmetic 3–4 forms → 2) plus PA-17.4 and PA-22.2. Web-verified per RR 11-2018/RMC 27-2018: 1601-E was replaced by the quarterly 1601-EQ in 2018; 1601-C remains the monthly compensation return. (2550M/Q and 1604-C/E verified current.)
- **DOLE D.O. 174 taxonomy mis-attribution (W555 ×3):** the probationary/project/seasonal employment taxonomy was attributed to "DOLE Department Order 174" — web-verified that D.O. 174-17 regulates contracting arrangements only; the classifications live in Labor Code Arts. 295–296 (probationary max 6 months = Art. 296), terminations in Art. 297. Repaired with a cross-reference keeping D.O. 174-17 for the W269 promodizer contracting regime; W1682's outsourcing-context DO 174 usage verified correct.
- **Register/canon alignments (11 spots):** W584 claimed "~150 DC staff on duty per shift per DC" — double the entire 600-DC-HC canon (4 DCs × 150 total across two shifts) and ~5× its own step-3 allocation → repaired in register + background, and its DC-Manager total re-derived (~700 h/year, was ~500); W976 staffing "~2–3 hours/day" vs its own ~30–40 selections/store/**month** → ~20–60 min/day; W1694 "~250–350 component structures/year" vs the 10–15 new stores/year (W1427) + ~30–40 remodels/year (W109.2) canons → ~40–60/year with the hours chain re-derived; W2198 "~400–500 deliveries/day" vs the ~10,000/month delivery canon (PA-56.1/56.2/56.3) → ~330–350/day; W1358 "5-day standard onboarding (W19.1)" vs W15's own 2–3-day canon → aligned; W1447 "~15 min/day" vs its own 5 min × 3 deliveries/week → per week; W5374 Volume "~300–800 onboardings" clarified as portfolio total vs its own "~50/yr".

## 2026-08-29 — Consistency review #53: semantic batch 10 (104 workflows, 993 → 1,097 of 5,363 read; 20.4% coverage) — 30 defect spots repaired across 13 classes (fabricated SEC "GAN" filing removed with title cascade, RA 9275→RA 8749 refrigerant mis-attribution ×8 with W4306 title cascade, W273 endless-aisle volume re-anchored to the W545 special-orders canon), all guarded

**Batch 10** (104 workflows, seed 5454, 13-per-family expansion-weighted, excluding all 993 prior W-ids; 76 distinct VS, 54 VS-53+; 93 PA files), pre-screened (zero flags) then read in full.

- **Fabricated SEC filing removed: "General Affiliate Notice (GAN)"** — W481 presented GAN as one of BuildRight's four annual SEC filings (title, filing calendar, data-gathering step, document-prep, review, pain point, time estimate, staffing) and PA-15.1's PAS-24 note referenced it. Web verification found no such SEC filing exists — the real affiliate/beneficial-ownership disclosure lives **inside the GIS** (per SEC.gov.ph/VCM Law/Grant Thornton); GAN content folded into the GIS beneficial-ownership declaration; title cascade repaired (header, TOC anchor, criticality register).
- **RA 9275 → RA 8749 ×8 spots (W4306 title cascade)** — refrigerant recovery/charging was attributed to the Clean **Water** Act; W4306 even glossed "RA 9275 Clean Air Act" (the Clean Air Act is RA 8749). Fixed across W4299 (×2), W4306 (title + 4 body spots), PA-143.1 (1 straggler), register row, TOC anchor. Remaining RA 9275 usages verified legitimate (wastewater/spill/Clean-Water contexts).
- **W273 endless-aisle volume re-anchored** — "10–20 per store per day" implied 60,000–120,000 orders/month, ~3–4× the canonical ~28,000/month total special orders (W545) and ~10% of all store labor; repaired to ~1–2 per store per **week** (~8,000–17,000/month, a proper subset of W545's total), Staffing/Time lines and the AP-FTE figure aligned (Check 47 initially caught the per-store/per-chain unit mismatch in my own first repair — re-repaired to per-week rate).
- **Canon/register alignments:** W1336's "~84,000 returns/month (~3%)" → the W1627 canon ~56,000/month (~2%), with the fraud-count line (2,800–4,500/month) and the "double the chain average" threshold gloss corrected (the PHP 60–100M annual-loss figure, checked both ways, coheres with the 56K base); W1452's B2C "55% of revenue" → ~60% (30% trade + 10% corporate B2B canon); W596's and two swept PA-05 instances of "30-person Supply Chain team" → the §3.3 register's 40; W1361's "~20–30 bank accounts" reconciled to W309's 15–20 treasury accounts plus settlement accounts.
- **Internal coherence:** W596's Frequency "~150–250 exceptions/week" contradicted its own Volume (250–400/month = 60–93/week) → 60–90/week; W1400's "~55 license expirations/year" contradicted the LTO 5-year validity and its own "~11 renewals/year" → 55 tracked / ~11 expiring per year.
- **Fragments/mis-pastes/typos:** W491's control line "operational: to file public financial statements" (pain-point fragment) → real tiering/watchlist control; W2706's scope-creep pain point (quotation boilerplate in a returns workflow) → condition-dispute risk — the literal-sweep then found 7 more instances adjudicated **legitimate** (quotation/project workflows where scope-creep is a real risk), so that literal was withdrawn (the batch-9 marketplace precedent); W4535's "transfer-prricing" typo.
- **Verified sound in the batch** (no action): 2/3 stockholder vote for capital increase (W1726); SENA/NLRC ladder; LTO restriction codes RC-2/RC-3 (W1400); TRAIN tables + 1601C + 2316-by-Jan-31 (W2764); RTPWB regional wage orders (W3310); PCAB cascading-license obligation (W4829); PCNC/BIR qualified-donee status (W4506); anti-islanding rules and BESS (W3461); IPMVP M&V (W3747); DTI price-freeze/anti-hoarding under the Price Act (W5189); OT security and IT/OT/HSE drills (W5425/W5429); PFRS 9 ECL on recourse (W5412); IFRS 16 lessor model and SLB PFRS 15 sale test (W4421); the §11.4 hires canon quoted correctly (W43, W3772); volume chains (5,000 replenishment orders = 5–8% exceptions, W596; 56,000-break math, W717's 27,048 training hours; CLV math exact in W1452).
- **Guard (Check 62):** 17 new retired literals; the first run caught 9 live instances (2 stale team sizes repaired; 7 scope-creep adjudicated legitimate and the literal withdrawn). Teeth verified by synthetic injection (caught, restored clean) — **0 hits across all 569 PA files**. One own-repair was itself caught by Check 47's unit-coherence rule (per-store/per-day vs per-month chain total) and re-repaired to a per-week rate — the guard stack working as designed.

Safe Spaces 10+-employee threshold, 2-notice rule, SENA (W5497 — exemplary); separation-pay 18.75-day DOLE formula and NIRC §32(B)(6) exemptions (W1385); Art. 83–85 meal/rest breaks with 200 × 29 × 3 = 17,400 events/day; RA 9275 interceptors, BFP FSIC; PhilHealth 5% (2.5/2.5) and Pag-IBIG PHP 100+100; DOLE-accredited SENA/NLRC arbitration ladder; GHS 16-section SDS and DENR-EMB CIR under RA 6969; PCI-PFI/BSP/NPC breach; AQL sampling; EDI 850/856/810; punchout cXML/OCI; DC1–DC4 locations vs profile; lumber species/2×4-dimension practice; W5509's lost-sales math (15–35K events = 2–6/store/day; 0.4–1% × 5.19B = 20–50M/month).

## 2026-08-29 — Consistency review #51: semantic batch 8 (104 workflows, 785 → 889 of 5,363 read) — 24 defect spots repaired across 8 classes (3 pain-point-fragments-as-controls, 3 statutory fixes incl. NOLCO and OT rates, 7 missing `### Steps` headers, garbled "SALSBAC", register-stale hire rates), all guarded

**Batch 8** (104 workflows, seed 5252, 13-per-family expansion-weighted, excluding all 785 prior W-ids; 75 distinct VS touched, 56 of them VS-53+; 93 PA files), pre-screened (zero flags) then read in full.

- **Statutory fixes:**
  - **W590 NOLCO mis-stated in both halves** — "3 years for small taxpayers, unlimited for large taxpayers" → the rule is **3 consecutive years, extended to 5 years for FY2020–2021 losses of non-large taxpayers per CREATE (RR 25-2020)**; there is no unlimited carryforward (verified against Grant Thornton/PwC/SGV).
  - **W1428 project employment cited Labor Code Article 294** (regular/casual employment) → **Article 297**.
  - **W1032 overtime rates quoted day-work rates as OT premiums** — "130% for rest days … 200% for regular holiday overtime" → **169% rest-day OT (130% day rate + 30% OT premium) and 260% regular-holiday OT (200% + 30%)**, in both the background and the payroll-calculation step; plus "18 regular holidays" → "~18 holidays (regular and special)".
- **Pain-point fragments pasted as operational controls** (the review #44 W449 class, 3 more instances): W199's "operational: as evidence", W590's "operational: (eFPS) experiences frequent downtime…", W732's "operational: is not always accessible or up-to-date…" → each replaced with the workflow's real control (DC Dispatch monitoring gate; Chief Accountant provision-journal approval; TIN-validation gate with AP hold).
- **Seven missing `### Steps` headers** — W4762's steps table followed its field table with no header, and a new structural rule found six more hidden in h3 sub-sections (PA-03.2 ×2, PA-04.1 ×2, PA-17.2 ×2); all given the header (11 descriptive-phase-header variants adjudicated as legitimate house style).
- **Register-stale hire rates** (§11.4 canon: ~1,200–1,600 hires/year = 100–133/month): W372's "~100–150 new hires/month" (×2 + its derived 1,500–2,250 h/year), W846's "~200/month given ~30% turnover" (with its 2,400-completions and 4,500-employee refresher base — the latter also stale vs the 5,800 store headcount, incl. the 1.5 × 4,500 = 6,750 h chain), W1500's "~15–25/month" (contradicting its own ~200–300-in-program Volume), and PA-27.1's license-allocation chain (10 min × 100–150 + 50–70 ≈ 25–37 h/month → 100–130 basis ≈ 25–33 h/month; totals 360–460 h/year).
- **Internal-coherence repairs:** W78's "~5–8% of total revenue" was impossible against its own contract math (20–40 bids × PHP 0.5–20M ≈ <1.3%) and the VS-46 canon (~3–5% government + institutional) → "a subset of the ~3–5% (VS-46)"; **W1547's Time-Estimate total** (~140–160 h/year) contradicted its own line items (100 h/quarter × 4 + 25 + 16 ≈ 440) → ~440 hours/year with staffing share 40% → 20–25%; **W3190/W3186's garbled "SALSBAC"** (×3 spots) → "sale-and-leaseback" (the VS-97 canon, incl. W3187's own title); **W216's "(W15.3)"** → "(profile §15.3)" (the retention section, not workflow W15).
- **Verified sound in the batch** (no action): RA 9184/PHILGEPS/ABC bidding mechanics; RA 9994/RA 10754 SC-PWD 20%+VAT-exemption with the 56,000–140,000/month volume consistent with W1627's 56,000-returns anchor; BIR CAS PTU/audit-trail immutability; CREATE RCIT 25%/FCIT 20%; RR 02-2013 TP benchmarking; PFRS 16/23; RA 9275 Clean Water Act interceptor; BFP FSIC; SWP/9D/9G/SVEG visa categories; CBA 5-year term with 60-day pre-expiry notice; RA 10364; DOLE OSH; Volume chains cross-verified (5.19B monthly revenue = 62.3B/12, 2.8M POS txn, ~205 leases/deposits/accounts, 600 DC staff, 29/store, ~150–230 new SKUs/month matching the 1,750–2,800/year canon, 400–600 TEU/month).
- **Guard (Check 62) extended:** 14 new retired literals (the mis-pasted controls, statutory forms, stale hire rates, SALSBAC, etc.) and the **steps-header structural rule** (a steps table must not directly follow a field-table row) — which surfaced the six additional hidden instances on its first run; workflow-attribution in the quote-integrity rules now resets at named h3 sub-sections so nested steps tables can't shadow the parent workflow's step map. Teeth verified: a synthetic SALSBAC literal and a synthetic header-less steps table were both caught, then restored clean — **0 hits across all 569 PA files**.

## 2026-08-29 — Consistency review #50: semantic batch 7 (104 workflows, 681 → 785 of 5,363 read) — the automation-keyword clip family closed at its true extent by a new general quote-integrity guard (884 mid-word clips + 50 '-logy' mis-repairs repaired); 16 batch-defect spots repaired across 9 classes, all guarded

**Batch 7** (104 workflows, seed 5151, 13-per-family expansion-weighted, excluding all 681 prior W-ids; 74 distinct VS touched, 55 of them VS-53+; 90 PA files), audited under the extended Check 62 guard with a mechanical pre-screen (typo tokens, doubled words, malformed rows — zero flags) before the full read.

- **The clip family at its true extent.** The batch read kept surfacing keyword quotes that are mid-word tails of longer step words — `of "count Manager/Customer Success…"` (Account), `of "counts"` (accounts), `of "file, including their store locations"` (profile), `of "file §7"` (profile), `of "counting and tax"` (accounting), `of "Accountant ensures…"` (Revenue Accountant). A new general step-verified audit (every `System … of "QUOTE" (replaces manual Step N)` bullet must quote its referenced step's Activity cell) swept the whole corpus and found the family at **884 spots** — account(s)/discount(s)→"count(s)", profile→"file", catalog→"log" (`of "log — stores in host cities"`), center→"enter" (`of "enter of Gravity"`), analogous→"logous", recalculates→"calculates", dispatches→"dispatch", checks→"check" … — every one mechanically re-quoted to the full in-step word(s) from its own referenced step.
- **The review #45/#47 '-logy' repairs were themselves 50× wrong.** The earlier clip repair expanded every `-logy/-logies` tail to "technology/technologies", but the clipped words were often **Metrology** (24 spots: `of "technology Manager…"` where the step says "Metrology Manager" in PA-115.x), methodology (PA-112.x, PA-39.x, PA-17.x, PA-35, PA-42, PA-79, PA-96, PA-97, PA-25, PA-21, PA-15, PA-11, PA-108), typologies (PA-125.x fraud `of "technologies per channel"`), toxicology (SDS), genealogy (kit lot/serial), apology (service-credit remediation) — all 50 restored to the step's true word. Plus W12B's three mis-generated bullets (quoted a non-existent in-store return flow), an em-dash quote variant (W558), and a `" … "`-abbreviation quote (W1543) re-quoted verbatim.
- **Batch semantic defects repaired (16 spots, 9 classes):**
  - **W347 E-commerce audit: all four step citations misdirected** — price parity pointed at W72 (Employee Performance Management!) → **W2237** Cross-Channel Price Consistency; fulfillment at W77 (BIR Tax Audit Response) → **W19** home-delivery fulfillment; payment integrity at W215 (returns reverse logistics) → **W267** ecommerce payment reconciliation; PII access-control test at W73 (system-upgrade testing) → **W1408** ERP access review & SoD audit; the marketplace-reconciliation step gained its missing **W724** citation.
  - **W204**: `PHP 500–2,000 per trip × ~50–100/week = PHP 100,000–800,000/week` — the product endpoints only cohere month-normalized (25–200K/week × 4) → unit corrected to **/month**.
  - **W872**: Frequency "~20–30 RFQs per month" contradicted the workflow's own annual formula (`30 RFQs × 20 h + 10 tenders × 50 h = 1,100 h/year`) and single-Procurement-Manager staffing by ~10× → **~2–3 RFQs per month** (≈30/year, exactly consistent with the formula).
  - **W1443**: group-moderation chain used 10 groups against the Volume field's ~200 contractor groups and the per-Store-Manager staffing → **30 min/group/day × ~200 groups (~30 min/day per Store Manager) = ~36,500 h/year**, annual estimate ~4,800 → **~40,000 distributed hours**.
  - **W1611**: per-occurrence effort omitted step 4's 30 min (10+30+30+~~30~~+10) → **1.83 h** (was 1.33), annual **92–147 h** (was 66.7–107).
  - **W755**: "~10–12 LP analysts chain-wide" vs the §3.3 register → **the 20 Regional LP Officers**.
  - **W1033**: stale "Supply Chain team (31 at HQ)" vs the rebalanced register → **40 per profile §3.3**; Time Estimate total (~6–10 h/week) contradicted the Staffing section (~3–5) → aligned at **~3–5 h/week**.
  - **W167**: recycling volume vs revenue incoherent by ~10× (5–10 t/store/month vs PHP 5–15K/store/year) → **~1–2 t/store/month** and **PHP 15,000–40,000/store/year** (roughly break-even against segregation labor — the sustainability-driven-not-profit-driven conclusion preserved).
  - **Three "(personnel safety logs)" wrong CTL-27 glosses** (W5260, W5263, and the PA-184.2 rebuilding workflow) → their real PA operating controls **CTL-780/CTL-783**; plus W3945's malformed `** ungated-build risk**` bold label.
- **Verified sound in the batch** (no action): statutory chains throughout — IPRA RA 8371 FPIC via NCIP (3–9 months), RA 11898 EPR (5,000–8,000 t plastic POM, 20%→80% targets, PHP 5–20M penalties), RCOA/RES/WESM/ERC retail competition, RA 9513 net metering with 4–6-year payback, RA 8749 emission testing, TRAIN tip-withholding + SSS/PhilHealth/Pag-IBIG base inclusion, NPC 72-hour breach window, PCC RPM/MAP/MFN clause discipline, PFRS 15 contract combination/cumulative catch-up, TESDA DTS/NC II, DENR EMB ECC/CNC, AMLC structuring/STR, LGU reclassification via Sanggunian ordinance, CAAP hazmat air-cargo limits, PHILGEPS Red/Gold. Volume anchors cross-verified: 2.8M POS txn/month = ~93K/day = W545's 28,000 special orders at ~1%; 42,900 ecommerce orders (8,580 = 20% warranty registrations); ~9,500 AP invoices/month total (6,715 merchandise + 2–3K non-PO per §10.2); 7.2M inventory parameter sets; 6,762 HC; ~1,200–1,600 hires (§11.4); 5 entities; ~205 accounts/deposits; PHP 62.3B × 1.5% = 935M shrinkage line.
- **Guard (Check 62) extended with the general quote-integrity rules** — containment (the quote must appear in the referenced step, bold/ellipsis/case/whitespace-normalized) and word-boundary (at least one occurrence must not sit inside a longer word), subsuming the piecemeal logy/countant/footprint clip regexes; 12 adjudicated noun-phrase summary bullets (vintage hand-authored style) allowlisted per (file, workflow); 6 new retired literals from this review's repairs. Teeth verified: a synthetic mid-word clip and a synthetic non-contained quote were both caught, then restored clean — **0 hits across all 569 PA files**.

Coverage registry `07-methodology/semantic-audit-coverage.txt` grown to **785 W-ids (14.6%)**; the batch was not zero-findings, so **the sampling loop continues per its directive**.

## 2026-08-29 — Consistency review #49: semantic-coverage lift — corpus-wide citation census + 200-workflow batch (481 → 681 of 5,363 read); 43 defect spots repaired across 8 classes, all guarded

**Census (machine, full corpus):** 110 distinct statutory-citation forms swept; ~100 adjudicated correct (DO 183-18, DAO 29-1992, D.O. 13, RA 10607/9006/10863/11210/10951, RR 2-98/3-98, SEC MC 28, RA 9501/5487/10623/10644/11232/11521/8371, …). Four live mis-citation classes (20 spots) repaired pre-batch: "DTI DAO 2" ×2 → Consumer Act RA 7394 basis (PA-13.1); "RR 19-2020" ×17 → RR 02-2013 house TP canon (PA-17.2, PA-18.1, PA-18.3, PA-72.1, PA-72.3, PA-97.2, PA-97.3); "RA 11199" → RA 11111 SSS (PA-19.2); "per DAO 198" → DOLE OSH Standards as amended by D.O. 198-18 (PA-07.3).

**Machine-sweep remainder of the guarded class:** four "No. 19-2020" spelling variants the bare-literal pass missed (PA-17.2 ×2, PA-18.3, PA-18.1 — incl. the W235 "BIR RR No. 19-2020" spotted mid-batch-read) → RR 02-2013; "RA 9160 as amended by RA 10121" (PA-22.1, W730) → RA 10365 and RA 11521 — RA 10121 is the DRRM Act, and its two remaining usages (PA-76.2 disaster-preparedness) are correct.

**200-workflow stratified batch (seed 5050, excluding all 481 prior W-ids; 121 distinct VS, 94 VS-53+):** read in full — 196 of 200 sound; 4 defect workflows repaired:
- **W269 promodizer management (PA-19.1): RACI R-column scrambled** — all five steps' Responsible contradicted the prose actor ("Vendor submits…" R=Store Manager; "Store Admin creates…" R=Security Guard; "System provisions…" R=Vendor Account Mgr; "Promodizer logs…" R=Store Manager; "Store Manager reviews…" R=HR Admin) → R realigned to the prose actor per row.
- **W2141 warranty partner review (PA-53.3): external Accountable** — "VP Merchandising conducts quarterly performance review with partner" carried A=Insurance Partner while sibling steps use internal A → A=CFO.
- **"BFS" ×11 → BPS / DTI-BPS** — PA-01.3 used "DTI/BFS", "BFS/DTI", "DENR/BFS" and the inverted "(BFS, formerly BPS)" gloss (the Bureau of Philippine Standards' acronym is and was BPS; corpus canon "DTI-BPS" ×208); PA-30.3 "PS Mark from BFS"; PA-09.1 fabricated "DOE Bureau of Fire Standards and Regulations (BFSR)" for cylinder hydrostatic re-testing → "Philippine standards (DTI-BPS)" (VS-175 anchors cylinder re-qualification to DTI-BPS per RA 11592).

**Guard (Check 62) extended:** RETIRED_LITERALS grown by 21 entries (the four census classes + "No. 19-2020" + "amended by RA 10121" + six BFS-family forms + five W269 row-fragments + the W2141 external-A fragment); every literal teeth-verified via synthetic injection (caught, then restored clean; 0 hits across 569 PA files).

## 2026-08-29 — Inline-arithmetic audit of the Time Estimate & Staffing Implication paragraphs: 25 workflows' defective scaling chains repaired against their own steps/Frequency/Volume; new tool `audit-time-estimate-math.py`; validator Check 50 added (50 checks)

Check 49 proved every workflow carries a *finalized* Time Estimate section; nothing verified the arithmetic written *into* those paragraphs. A new whole-repo correctness pass re-derived every explicit inline computation of the form `<A> × <B> (=|≈) <result>` across the 5,382 Time Estimate and Staffing Implication sections — applying the unit conventions the house style licenses (min↔hours ÷/×60, sec→hours ÷3600, workday ×8 and workweek ×5/×7 alternates, month-length alternates, per-cadence annualization ×12/×4/×52/×365/×6, noun/suffix cancellation such as `2 hours/visit × 20–30 visits/month`, the canonical 200-store and 4-DC chain scalings, shared-factor inheritance, K/M endpoints, percentages as decimals) and hand-adjudicating every candidate hit against its workflow's own steps table, Frequency, and Volume. Findings and repairs — 25 workflows carried genuinely defective chains:

- **Minutes labelled hours (×60 class):** W1517/W1518 chain-wide consultation totals (`× 80 min average = ~6,400–9,600 hours/month` — the product is minutes, ≈ 107–160 hours); W2135 warranty-upsell cashier time (`45 sec × ~42,000/month = ~31,500 hours` → ≈ 525 hours).
- **Overstated products:** W2215 price-gap decisions (claimed ~140 h/month, true ~19); W2362 store-feedback triage (~140 → ~19); W2369 overdue-task follow-up (~140 → ~36); W2344 sample-display maintenance (~470 h/week → ~100–150 per its own 30–45 min/week step); W2463 vendor review ratings (~300–375 h/quarter → ~75–125); W2454 KPI framework (~120–160 h/year → ~55–90); W2141 extended-warranty governance (~80 → ~45); W2194 3PL policy review (~30 → ~18–20); W2469 cross-functional scorecard inputs (~25 h/quarter → ~9–10); W2246 coupon-fraud screening (~480 → ~810 h/month, understated); W1248 BOPIS security (~79,600 → ~95,800 person-hours/year, understated); W867 vendor-portal AP (420 → 500 h/month at the authored 3,000-invoice volume and 80/20 split; the without-portal contrast re-based to the derivable all-manual figure).
- **Double-multiplied chain-wide counts:** W1352 outbound pick verification multiplied the chain-wide ~600 picks/day by 4 DCs again (all three bullets 4× overstated; discrepancy resolution up to 10×).
- **Contradicted own Time Estimate / steps:** W1305 POS price-verification (~520 daily hours/store vs its own 10–15 min/day → ~60–90; chain-wide ~120,000 → ~23,000–40,000); W1486 staffing line (~20 h/month vs the workflow's own ~25–33 total); W586 DC-day KPI chain (intermediate 50–90 min vs its step sum 40–60, cascaded through the annual figures); W634/W635-class staffing undercounts; W1433 catch-weight recording (~200,000–333,000 → ~120,000–200,000 min/day); W2349 decommission removal (~150–250 → ~200–400 h/year); W2368 task-compliance oversight (~180 → ~255 h/year); W2186 space-productivity inputs re-worded to show the ×3-formats factor; W2676 LGU-database assessment re-based to ~1 hour/location × 209.
- **New tool [`07-methodology/audit-time-estimate-math.py`](07-methodology/audit-time-estimate-math.py)** — expression evaluator over the two section families: tokenizes quantity ranges with units and per-suffixes, folds `+`/`×` elementwise, verifies each comparison under any licensed combination of conventions (charitable by design — a chain clears if *some* consistent reading lands within tolerance, so survivors of the filter are genuinely suspect), and flags reversed numeric ranges. Default mode prints the full candidate list for human adjudication (accepted classes: house-style range padding ±35%, elapsed-window days/weeks reported verbatim per the finalization rules, hidden context factors stated elsewhere in the workflow); `--guard` is the strict zero-false-positive subset — single-run effort-time product chains whose midpoint is off ≥1.6× under no licensed convention, plus reversed ranges — which was tuned against the fully-adjudicated hit list and catches every defect class repaired above (verified by re-feeding the pre-repair lines).
- **Validator Check 50 (new):** runs the tool's `--guard` mode repo-wide; root-README tree line and methodology README bumped to 50 checks (Check 40 verifies); methodology README gains the tool row; the format guide carries a dated inline-arithmetic audit note documenting the licensed conventions and the accepted prose classes.

Canonical totals unchanged: **188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,363 workflows · 728 requirements · 808 controls · 6,762 headcount**. No steps tables, frequencies, volumes, cross-references, or controls were modified — only the defective Time Estimate / Staffing Implication figures and their shown math were corrected. After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **50 checks**.

## 2026-08-28 — Time-Estimate finalization pass: all 407 mechanical draft roll-ups completed to the house style (per-occurrence roll-up + Frequency-derived annualization); new tool `finalize-time-estimates.py`; validator Check 49 added (49 checks)

Closing the repo's last self-declared content backlog: the **407 `### Time Estimate` sections** still carrying `backfill-time-estimate.py`'s honest-draft marker ("Draft roll-up from per-step Durations … refine to the per-step range + annual figure house style"). The draft-roll-up count had walked 410 → 408 → 407 across prior sessions (only W424 had been hand-finalized, as the exemplar). Every one of the 407 is now a house-style paragraph, with **no fabricated figures** — each derives from its own workflow's authored fields:

- **118 auto-decided** (mechanically unambiguous): every Duration reads as per-occurrence effort and the Frequency carries an explicit occurrence count in the same unit — the roll-up sums the step Durations low+low / high+high and the annual figure multiplies by the Frequency count. Bare cadence words are auto-decided only for single pure cadence phrases ("Monthly", "Quarterly") without a competing cadence-marked Volume count; elapsed-day steps, recurring-rate steps ("30 min/week"), cadence-carried steps, automated/negligible steps, and unscaled per-unit steps are reported verbatim, never summed.
- **289 adjudicated one by one** (the genuinely mixed cases): compound cadences (W624's monthly cycle with per-visit steps and weekly/quarterly embedded cadences), per-unit Volume scaling (W1188's 2 hours/store × 200 stores), per-unit steps whose unit is the Frequency's own counted noun (W1619's 30 min/complaint), chain-wide per-store rates (W574 store closings × 200 × 365), elapsed-only timelines (the VS-35/VS-40 audit and turnover families), and honest event-driven frequencies that carry no annual figure (typhoon response, emergency capex). Every paragraph shows its scaling math inline ("~25 min per occurrence (…) × ~2,000–3,000 complaints/month ≈ 12,000–54,000 hours/year"), so each figure is re-derivable from the workflow's own content.

**Guardrails learned the hard way** (each a first-draft defect caught in audit before writing): a bare "Monthly" frequency cannot be multiplied through when the Volume carries a different occurrence unit (W624's visits); a Volume-scaled per-unit total must never be multiplied by an event count (W812 overstated ~10×); step cadences hiding in Activity prose make mixed-cadence workflows non-uniform (W1189); high-count volume-style frequencies (W464's ~400–600 TEUs/month) need occurrence-unit verification because multiple TEUs ride one entry; and a mid-number regex match ("15,000" → "000") once produced "≈ 0 occurrences/year".

- **New tool [`07-methodology/finalize-time-estimates.py`](07-methodology/finalize-time-estimates.py)** — three-stage pipeline kept for the record and future reruns: `--workbench` (classifies every remaining draft, auto-deciding the safe classes and flagging the rest with reasons), `--apply` (renders final paragraphs from a decisions file — structured decisions for auto cases, `text` overrides for adjudicated ones), `--check` (exit 1 while drafts remain). Idempotent; render-time guards for balanced parens and unit-consistent ranges.
- **Validator Check 49 (new): Time-Estimate finalization state** — errors on any recurrence of the retired `Draft roll-up from per-step Durations` literal in PA files, and on `### Time Estimate` section count ≠ workflow count (5,363). Root-README tree line and methodology README bumped to 49 checks (Check 40 verifies); methodology README gains the tool row; the format guide carries a dated finalization note documenting the derivation rules.
- **Cross-docs**: root README tree (`validate-repo.sh` count + new tool line), `07-methodology/README.md` (contents row, validate-repo.sh row extended with Check 49, backfill-time-estimate.py row notes the closure), `WORKFLOW-FORMAT-GUIDE.md` (finalization note ahead of the completeness note). CHANGELOG history entries mentioning the 407/408 backlog stand as the historical record.

Canonical totals unchanged: **188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,363 workflows · 728 requirements · 808 controls · 6,762 headcount**. No steps tables, frequencies, volumes, cross-references, or controls were modified — only the 407 Time Estimate section bodies were rewritten from their own workflow data. After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **49 checks**.

## 2026-08-28 — Consistency review #33: statutory-citation correctness sweep #4 — Competition Act number restored (RA 10677 → RA 10667), six further mis-citations repaired, house-spelling normalized; validator Check 46 guard extended (48 checks)

A whole-repo review for consistency, correctness and completeness — running with the validator fully green at 48 checks — re-audited the repo's ~100 distinct Republic Act / PD / DO / DAO / RR citations, the summary-doc figures and footer-history integrity, and the spelling house-style. Findings and repairs:

- **Philippine Competition Act number restored — RA 10677 → RA 10667 (25 spots).** Review #31's sweep flipped the act number repo-wide on the claim that "the Philippine Competition Act is RA 10677, not RA 10667" — backwards: **RA 10667** (signed 21 July 2015) is the Philippine Competition Act that creates the PCC (RA 10668 is the Right-of-Way Act; RA 10677 is not the Competition Act). Corrected across VS-129 (README + three PA files incl. the PA header quotes), PA-57.1 (×2, incl. the 2026-06-20 rework note), PA-76.2 (×3, incl. the hyphenated `RA-10677-flavored` form), PA-172.1, `workflows/README.md`, `value-stream-index.md`, `workflow-dependency-map.md`, `workflow-gap-analysis.md` (×3), and `workflow-system-touchpoint-map.md`. Validator Check 46's retired-literal guard — which reviews #31/#32 had taught the wrong number — now retires the `RA 10677` form instead; the methodology README's guard description re-pointed; dependency map **v4.15**, touchpoint map **v82.0**, and the index footer dated accordingly.
- **PA-100.1**: `commercial arbitration per ADR Act RA 876` — RA 876 (1953) is the Arbitration Law, not the ADR Act (RA 9285, 2004); now cites both correctly.
- **PA-08.1 (×3, automation fragment kept verbatim with the step)**: age-restricted ID checks cited RA 7610 (the child-protection act) — age-restricted sale bans run per **RA 9211 §9** (tobacco) / **PD 1619** (volatile solvents).
- **`erp-requirements.md` COM-001**: `DOLE DO 53-03` → the drug-free-workplace guidelines are DOLE **D.O. 53-04** (the catalog's own W4458 title).
- **PA-17.3**: the e-invoicing example cited `RR 13-2018 (SAF-T)` — SAF-T is an OECD format, not a BIR standard; e-receipts/e-invoicing run per **RR 8-2020** (NIRC §237 as amended by TRAIN).
- **PA-31.3**: hazardous-release notification paired `PD 1586, RA 6969` — PD 1586 is the EIS/ECC system; releases run under RA 6969 + its IRR alone.
- **VS-192 README + PA-192.1**: EVIDA's IRR cited as `DAO 2023-05` — EVIDA (RA 11697) is DOE-administered and its IRR is a DOE department circular, while DENR DAO 2023-05 is the EPR Act (RA 11898) IRR; re-pointed to "its DOE IRR".
- **Dating + footer-history repairs**: review #31's "(2020)" dating of the LPG Industry Regulation Act corrected to **2021** (Check 46 comment + classification v7.40 footer); the v7.39 register-footer paragraph that review #31's v7.40 edit absorbed inline restored as its own standalone note; classification register bumped **v7.41** for the W4710 title normalization.
- **House-spelling normalization** to the dominant US spellings: dependant → dependent (23 spots, incl. the W4710 title / TOC-anchor / register-row cascade in VS-160), judgement → judgment (9, PA-157.3), fulfilment → fulfillment (1, VS-140 README), labelling → labeling (3, PA-115.1/.2).

**Validator Check 46 extended** with the seven newly retired literals (`RA 10677`, `ADR Act RA 876`, `age-restricted items per RA 7610`, `DO 53-03`, `RR 13-2018 (SAF-T)`, `PD 1586, RA 6969`, `IRR (DAO 2023-05)`); 48 checks unchanged. Totals unchanged 188 VS / 569 PA / 5,363 WF / 728 Req / 808 CTL / 6,762 HC; validator green 0 errors / 0 warnings.

The act is **RA 11592**, signed March 2020; RA 10617 sits in the late-2013 numbering, years before the 17th/18th-Congress LPG bills were even filed, so it cannot be the act. This reverses consistency review #29's adjudication (which had itself reversed review #13's `RA 10862`); the register row carries a v7.40 note.
- **Philippine Competition Act cited as `RA 10667`** — 25 spots across VS-129 (README H1 + body, all three PAs), value-stream-index ×2, workflows README, dependency map, touchpoint map, gap-analysis ×3, VS-76 PA-76.2 ×3, VS-57 PA-57.1 ×2, VS-172 PA-172.1. The PCA is **RA 10677** (signed 21 July 2015).
- **Hazardous-waste management cited as `DAO 2013-22` with a US-style 90-day accumulation limit** — VS-73 (PA-73.1 ×2, PA-73.2 ×6 incl. the step/risk/control machinery, README ×3), PA-22.1, PA-114.3. DAO 2013-22 is the mercury CCO; hazardous-waste management under RA 6969 runs on **DAO 2004-36** (Procedural Manual), which PA-22.2 already cites correctly for its **6-month** storage limit — the 90-day countdown machinery in PA-73.2/VS-73 README contradicted that same document set. Re-based to 180-day (6-month) throughout, pickup trigger day 75 → day 150.
- **BIR transfer-pricing benchmarking cited as `RR 34-2022`** (PA-36.3 W2582 governance layer) — no such regulation exists; the BIR transfer-pricing regulations are **RR 02-2013**, which FIN-049/PA-17.2 already cite correctly.
- **Collateral perfection cited as `RA 9266`** (VS-188 README floor-plan security agreement) — RA 9266 is the Architecture Act; replaced with **Act No. 1508 (Chattel Mortgage Law) / RA 11057 (Personal Property Security Act)**, the actual security-interest instruments.

**Validator Check 46 extended**: its retired-literal guard now also covers `RA 10862`, `RA 10617`, `RA 10667`, `DAO 2013-22`, and `RR 34-2022` so no surface can regress to any of them. ## 2026-08-26 — Post-catalog workflow-level gap fills, batch 5: 1 workflow added (W5510) — supplier service-fee billing & account deduction for store-rendered services (barcode labels & promotional collaterals)

A supplier-expense-model review found the two existing supplier-recovery paths each own a different money flow — co-op marketing funds (W513 in VS-03 / W1796–W1801 in VS-39) settle *pre-agreed campaign reimbursement*, and compliance chargebacks (W245) settle *penalties for non-performance* — while **per-event, store-rendered services billed to the supplier's account** (printing barcode/price labels for supplier items, producing promotional collaterals, re-applying source tags the vendor promised but shipped without) had no owner: 'service fee'/'supplier billing' appeared in zero merchandise-supplier PA files as dedicated headers, with only the concessionaire analog W5507 (VS-07.1) modeling the pattern. **W5510** fills the gap inside **VS-15.1**, transposing the W5507 concessionaire service-fee pattern onto merchandise suppliers and settling through the W770 AP debit-memo mechanism: a contracted per-event fee schedule with free allowances versioned on the trade agreement (W688) behind a vendor opt-in flag (events for non-enrolled vendors route to the Category Manager to absorb under co-op funding or decline); chargeable events auto-logged at source (W4490/W181 print runs, W4488 barcode changes, W1796/W1798 campaign collateral, W4498 source-tag re-application) as evidence-linked records **provisional until service completion**, with a co-op coverage flag preventing double-billing against W1799 settlement; a monthly itemized statement in the vendor portal (W705) with free-allowance balances and a 5-business-day dispute window before posting (contested lines withheld, not auto-credited; expedite-privilege suspension while a dispute is open); settlement as W770 debit memos (deduction type "service fee") netted at the next W556 payment run with a one-to-one credit-note matching guard, an AR invoice fallback (W8/W108) for consignment-only/seasonal suppliers with no open AP balance, and a cash-settlement route where the vendor requires a BIR 2307 withholding certificate; separate service-revenue/output-VAT treatment per W473 (distinct from W245 penalty income and W1799 co-op income); and a quarterly fee-income-vs-service-cost recovery review with renewal-time-only schedule changes. Canonical totals move **5,362 → 5,363 workflows** (188 value streams · 569 process areas unchanged; family subtotal: Finance 772 → 773; VS-15 42 → 43; PA-15.1 18 → 19). W5510 ships **unclassified** per repo convention; `classify-workflows.py` regeneration proposes **Tier 1** (the `barcode` core-transactional keyword, W5507 precedent — recovery-integrity driven rather than go-live blocking, so it warrants explicit confirmation review), making the proposal register **6 Tier 1 / 8 Tier 2 across 14**. Cross-docs reconciled: PA-15.1 TOC/footer, VS-15 README, value-stream-index (architecture line, VS row, Finance subtotal, grand total, PA bullet), workflows/README (quick stats, classified-total & proposed-register notes, family heading, VS row, subtotal line), criticality classification v7.39 (banner, proposed/coverage rows, batch-5 note), criticality-proposed regenerated, dependency map v4.14 (intro counts + new-edge note), touchpoint map v81.0 (footer reconciliation), gap analysis (batch-5 note), internal-controls-matrix (W5510 appended to CTL-284), requirement-workflow-matrix v77 (FIN-072 supporting list + inventory line), executive summary, format guide, and the root README. After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports its standard green across all checks.

## 2026-08-26 — Consistency review #30: per-store-rate ↔ chain-wide-total unit drift (~37 surfaces across 10 PAs), steps-table row/header/ordering defects (9), validator Checks 47–48 added (48 checks)

A whole-repo consistency review — running with the validator green at 46 checks — targeted a figure-drift family no structural check could see: **Frequency/Volume/Time-Estimate surfaces pairing a per-store cadence with a chain-wide total whose unit (or arithmetic) does not cohere with the rate**, plus a steps-table rendering family invisible to Check 29 (which only validates rows that already start with `|`). Four repaired clusters:

1. **Store receiving-cadence cluster (W723/W745/W18/W109 + the PA-22.1 staffing echo; 13 surfaces).** PA-07.3 quoted 2–3 trucks/store/**day** + 1–2 DSD/day = "~600–1,000 truck arrivals/day chain-wide" and "200 stores × ~10 receiving events/day = ~2,000/day", contradicting the canonical profile §7.1 cadence (2–3 DC deliveries/store/**week**, ~500–600 DSD receipts/**month** chain-wide, per PA-22.1's own staffing row). Re-derived: **~80–120 truck arrivals/day** (DC ~400–600/week + DSD ~500–600/month + transfers/returns), **3–5 receiving events/store/week** (~800/week ≈ ~115/day chain-wide), DSD 2–3/store/**month**, and W723's time estimate re-split into routine daily management + per-truck minutes across the ~3–5 trucks actually received each week.
2. **Per-week rate quoted with per-year total family (10 surfaces).** Ten workflows paired a per-store **per-week/per-month** rate with a chain-wide "/year" total that was 4–12× off: home-delivery requests (W4A-era, ~4,000–8,000), lumber quality disputes (~1,000–2,000), in-store financing applications (~10,000–16,000, plus the `200 × ~65/month = ~13,000/year` Volume row), color-match requests (~8,000–12,000), renovation material calculations (~3,000–5,000), contractor plan reviews (~1,000–2,000), roofing estimations (~2,000–4,000), vendor demo events (~400–800), and project shopping lists (~4,000–8,000) — all totals re-based to **/month**, matching their own rates (e.g. 200 stores × ~65/month ≈ 13,000/month). Two carried the same typo class ("calculatens"/"estimatens" → calculations/estimations).
3. **Dropped-multiplier / double-multiplier family (12 surfaces).** Opening/closing staff-hours dropped the hours multiplier (200 × 365 with no × 0.75/× 1.0 hr → "5,475"/"7,300"; now **~54,750/~73,000 staff-hours/year**); the evacuation people-at-risk figure dropped the × 200 ("~18,000+"; now **~99,000+ people/operating day chain-wide**, staff restated per-store); promo terminal verification over-multiplied ("~600–1,800 hours/week" vs the line's own 90–270 min/store/week × 200 → **~300–900**); promo setup density disagreed with its own chain total (20–30/store/month → **9–13**); shelf-tag printing double-multiplied the chain-wide 10–25 daily price changes ("2,000–5,000 tags/day across all stores" → most stores see a few tag changes/week, with markdown/promo waves adding **~2,000–5,000 tag prints/month**); trade-in cadence (~1–2/store/**week** → **~1/month**, 2 surfaces) and bulk-negotiation cadence (~3–4/store/**week** → **/month**) realigned to their ~150–250 and ~600–800/month chain totals; W139's overdue-call per-store minutes re-derived (6–12 → **4–8 min/store/month**).
4. **Steps-table integrity family (9 defects).** Five rows missing their leading table pipe — rendering outside their tables — re-piped (PA-05.2 W22 `9a` (also missing its trailing pipe), PA-17.1 rows `11`/`12`, PA-17.4 W9A `6a` and `16c`); two header typos canonicalized (PA-57.2 `Role (R))` → `Role (R)`, PA-170.3 `Role(A)` → `Role (A)`); three mis-ordered step rows re-sequenced to the house (num, letter)-ascending convention (W922 row 2 below row 1; W35 `14a` after `14`; W9A `16c` after `16a`/`16b`).

**Validator Checks 47–48 added**: Check 47 (per-store-rate ↔ chain-total unit coherence: every same-line pairing of a "N–M per store per week/month/day/year" rate with a "~A–B …/unit chain-wide" total must coherce once normalized to events/month/200-store chain, effort/weight-denominated totals exempt; plus explicit `200 stores × N = M` store-math verification, raw or cadence-normalized) and Check 48 (steps-table row integrity: canonical header form, no bare `6a |`-class rows, (num, letter)-ascending step IDs with letter variants after their base row). Check-count self-descriptions raised to 48 (root-README tree line, methodology README row; the former `→`-line exemption in the Check-40 self-description scan was removed — it silently exempted the methodology README's own contents row). **No workflow IDs, titles, tier assignments, register-row counts, or canonical totals changed** (188 VS · 569 PA · 5,360 WF · 728 Req · 808 CTL · 6,762 HC). After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **48 checks**.

## 2026-08-26 — Consistency review #29: semantic tender-mix / fleet / SKU figure drift + 4 statutory-citation correctness defects repaired; validator Check 46 added (46 checks)

A whole-repo review for consistency, completeness, correctness and unambiguity — running with the validator fully green at 45 checks — hunted defect classes the machine checks structurally **cannot** see (every existing check validates counts, IDs, anchors, tables, names, or list hygiene; none validated that quoted *volumes and statutes agree with the canonical operating parameters*). The review audited the tender-mix, fleet, and assortment parameter families cross-document (re-deriving each from `model-company-profile.md`), swept per-day/per-month conversions, and re-audited every distinct Republic Act, Presidential Decree, and DOLE Department Order citation against the statute each is invoked for (the review-#13 statutory audit had verified ~85 distinct numbers but its RA 10862 adjudication is reversed here — see below). Four defect clusters repaired:

1. **E-wallet tender-mix drift (W261, VS-15 PA-15.2, 3 spots).** W261's Volume band was built on inputs no other document shares: "~45,000 ecommerce orders/month × 40% e-wallet = ~18,000" (canonical order base is ~42,900) and "~560,000 POS transactions/month × 30% e-wallet = ~168,000" — a POS base that is 20% of the canonical 2.8M with a share that is double the canonical ~15% (PA-08.2/W1268 and W260 both quote 2.8M × ~15% = ~420,000/month). Rebased to the canonical mix: **~42,900 × 40% = ~17,000 ecommerce + ~2.8M × ~15% = ~420,000 POS = ~437,000 e-wallet transactions/month total** (Volume row, Volume-complexity pain point, and Staffing-implication mention).
2. **Order-of-magnitude per-day slip (W1268 Background, PA-08.2).** "approximately 42,000 transactions per day" alongside the same paragraph's correct 420,000/month and PHP 756M/month — 420,000 ÷ 30 ≈ **14,000/day**; the sentence now shows the conversion. (Checked repo-wide: the only other per-day conversions — PA-07.1's 467/day and W537's 167/day/store — derive correctly.)*
3. **Non-derivable POS hardware fleet (W265, VS-27 PA-27.1, 6 spots + a 7th in PA-29.3 caught by the new check's first run).** W265 quoted "~1,000 terminals", "4,000 PM events/quarter", and "~500 hours/quarter … ~125 hours each" while its own Time Estimate derives "3 terminals/store = ~1.5 hours/store" — i.e. 600 terminals and ~300 hours/quarter, matching the canonical 3-per-store × 200 = **600 POS terminals** (96 citations repo-wide; no 1,000-device fleet exists anywhere else). All six spots re-derived to the 600-terminal fleet (~600 terminal PM events/quarter; ~300 hours/quarter ÷ 4 specialists = ~75 hours each ≈ ~6 hours/week); the new Check 46 then flagged the same stale fleet figure in PA-29.3's barcode-to-channel sync step ("scanning at 1,000 terminals"), also repaired.*
4. **Assortment figure (W478, VS-17 PA-17.3).** The BIR Annual Inventory List Volume quoted "~80,000 active SKUs" against the canonical 35,000 active / ~55,000 item-master records (profile §6.1; 175 citations) — re-pointed to the canonical pair.

**Statutory-citation correctness (4 defects):**

- **LPG Industry Regulation Act cited as the wrong act number** — 19 live spots (VS-175 README, PA-175.1 ×10 incl. the W5059 title + TOC anchor, PA-175.2 ×3, criticality-classification register row, gap-analysis ×2) cited `RA 10862`; the act is **RA 10617** (2013). Consistency review #13's statutory audit had adjudicated RA 10862 correct — reversed here; the register row carries a v7.37 note. The one paired short title ("LPG Industry Regulatory Act") also aligned to the act's real short title (LPG Industry Regulation Act).
- **13th month pay cited as `RA 10691`** (PA-17.4 year-end pain point; PA-19.4 profit-sharing background) — the 13th-Month Pay Law is **PD 851** (1975), which the repo already cites correctly 6× elsewhere.
- **Phantom BIR ATC codes** (PA-17.3 W2307 validation & pain point) — "WI 010 for professional services, WI 028 for rental, WI 160 for goods" and "WI 010 … should be WI 011" are not the real withholding-income codes; corrected to the canonical set **WC 010** (professional/technical services to corporate vendors, 2%), **WI 010** (rentals, 5%), **WP 010** (purchases of goods, 1%).
- **Store-forklift statute misdirection** (PA-07.2 W438 forklift background) — forklifts in *retail* operations were cited to DOLE DO 13-98 (construction-industry OSH guidelines) plus an unidentifiable "D.O. 132-13"; re-anchored to the correct general-industry framework: **OSH Standards Rule 1160 series (Materials Handling and Storage, as amended by D.O. 198-18)**, with an explicit note that DO 13-98 does not apply to store operations.

Also verified clean this pass: the remaining ~75 distinct RA numbers re-confirmed correct (incl. RA 876 Arbitration Law, RA 9474 Lending Company Regulation Act, RA 9501 MSME Magna Carta, RA 10644 Go Negosyo Act, RA 11199 SSS Rationalization Act, PD 115 Trust Receipts Law, D.O. 53-03/53-04 drug-free workplace); the doubled-word sweep returns only false positives ("by by-laws", "remit-to to", "Build Build Build"); loyalty (600K), revenue (PHP 5.19B/62.3B), ecommerce (42,900 × PHP 3,500 ≈ 150M), vendor (800–1,000), PO (~1,200 merchandise), receipt (~72K/yr), rental-fleet (600–800 = 200 × 3–4), store-size, and staffing parameter families all reconcile; the "~28 employees" assessment pool (29 minus manager) and "~5,100 remaining trade accounts" (5,200 − 100 QBR) derive correctly. **Validator Check 46 added** (stale-literal guard: the retired tender-mix/fleet/SKU figures and superseded citation forms must not reappear in current-state prose; CHANGELOG and 'X → Y' change-notes exempt). Check-count self-descriptions raised to 46 (root-README tree line, methodology README row + footer); classification register footer v7.37. **No workflow IDs, titles-except-the-W5059-RA-number, tier assignments, register-row counts, or canonical totals changed** (188 VS · 569 PA · 5,360 WF · 728 Req · 808 CTL · 6,762 HC). ## 2026-08-25 — Post-catalog workflow-level gap fills, batch 3: 3 workflows added (W5505–W5507) — concession item catalog/barcode/label governance, concessionaire self-service price change with label-first propagation, and concession service-fee billing & cost recovery

A concessionaire-operating-model review found that **W177** (Vending & Concessionaire Management) — the single workflow the gap analysis relied on to close the in-store concessionaire/kiosk/vending candidate — modeled only agreement admin, a single throughput SKU, and monthly billing: item-level concession pricing, barcodes, labels, and any concessionaire self-service had no owner ('concession item', 'concessionaire price', 'concessionaire portal' appeared in zero PA files as dedicated headers). Three workflows fill the gap inside **VS-07.1**, transposing the VS-95 marketplace seller-portal pattern (W3140/W3141/W3142/W3152) in-store: **W5505** Concession Item Catalog, Barcode & Price-Label Onboarding & Governance (item-level concession SKUs with concessionaire-owner flags — distinct from W252 merchandise and W23 consignment, since concession goods never enter BuildRight inventory; GS1 barcodes accepted only after a chain-wide uniqueness check per W311 with collisions blocking activation, internal concession-range barcodes assigned otherwise per the W168/W169 pattern; barcode changes re-verified with label-reprint tasks; BuildRight-printed vs concessionaire-printed label ownership on the agreement; RA 7394 tag compliance; annual catalog reconciliation with ghost-SKU purge), **W5506** Concessionaire Self-Service Price Change Request, Approval & Store-Level Propagation (portal request with zero system effect until approval → automated guardrails: agreement change-limits/floors, undercut flag vs BuildRight comparables, automatic W468 DTI price-freeze rejection, VAT math → Store Manager approval for single-store items, Category Manager for chain-wide, 2-day SLA, auto-approval only within ±10%/no-format-change/no-active-dispute → an **unambiguous label-first cutover**: label batch releases T-1 16:00, scan/photo-confirmed swap by close, POS price activates 06:00 within 60 s with offline stale-price deferral, and a 05:30 auto-hold escalates any store with an unconfirmed swap — the POS price structurally can never lead the shelf tag (RA 7394); barcode-change cutover with 7-day alias ring; emergency rollback; 10-year audit trail feeding W177 commission settlement), and **W5507** Concession Service-Fee Billing & Cost Recovery (contracted per-event fee schedule recorded on the agreement — label printing, barcode changes, expedited/excess price changes — auto-logged as evidence-linked events provisional until service completion, aggregated into the monthly W177 invoice as a Service Fees section with a 5-business-day portal dispute window before finalization, separate service-revenue/VAT treatment per W473 with AR per W8/W108, and a quarterly fee-income-vs-service-cost recovery review feeding renewal). Canonical totals move **5,357 → 5,360 workflows** (188 value streams · 569 process areas unchanged; family subtotal: Sell & Serve 1,532 → 1,535; VS-07 144 → 147; PA-07.1 60 → 63). The three ship **unclassified** per repo convention; `classify-workflows.py` regeneration proposes **W5505/W5507 Tier 1** (the `barcode` core-transactional keyword — POS scan integrity) and **W5506 Tier 2**, so the proposal register now holds **3 Tier 1 / 8 Tier 2 across 11** pending a confirmation pass. Cross-docs reconciled: PA-07.1 TOC/footer, VS-07 README, value-stream-index (architecture line, VS row, Sell & Serve subtotal, grand total, PA bullet), workflows/README (quick stats, classified-total & proposed-register notes, family heading, VS row, subtotal line), criticality classification v7.36 (banner, proposed/coverage rows, batch-3 note), criticality-proposed regenerated, dependency map v4.11 (intro counts + new-edge note), touchpoint map v79.0 (footer reconciliation), gap analysis (batch-3 note + concessionaire rejected-candidate pointer), internal-controls-matrix (W5505–W5507 appended to CTL-259), requirement-workflow-matrix (GOV-020), executive summary, format guide, and the root README. ## 2026-08-25 — Consistency review #27: Controls-section rendering repair (3,349 dangling bullets + 78 paren imbalances + 35 junk segments + 26 mangled bold labels); validator Check 43 added (43 checks)

A whole-repo review for consistency, completeness, correctness and unambiguity — running with the validator fully green at 42 checks — hunted defect classes the machine checks structurally **cannot** see (every prior check validates counts, IDs, anchors or tables; none validated *list hygiene inside analysis sections*). Inspecting rendered workflow blocks found a systematic rendering-defect cluster in the `### Controls` sections, all inherited from the original `add-automation-controls.py` generator and invisible to GitHub readers as intended formatting:

1. **3,349 dangling `operational:` lines.** The generator emitted CTL bullets with a `- ` prefix but `operational:` items without one (`generate_controls` returns bare strings for the pain-point/pattern branches), so 3,349 Controls sections across 319 PA files rendered the operational controls as a stray paragraph glued below the bullet list. Repaired to the generator's own item form (`- operational: …`) by the new `07-methodology/fix-controls-bullets.py` (idempotent, `--check` mode integrated with the tool family).
2. **78 unbalanced-parenthesis Controls lines.** The generator's pain-point extraction regex (`mitigated by (.+?)(?:[.;]|$)`) truncated captures at the first period of "e.g."/"vs."/"No." and swallowed closing parens at `; operational:` joins — e.g. `…monitoring (links to VS-22; operational: …` missing its `)`. The unambiguous majority pattern (`X (links to VS-NN; operational:` — ~50 lines) is repaired mechanically by re-inserting `)` at the join; the remainder are part of cluster 3.
3. **35 junk segments — pain-point *descriptions* mislabelled as controls.** The same regex also captured bold labels and truncated risk text (e.g. `operational: reliability**: If the PA system fails during an emergency, lives are at risk`, `operational: downtime** — the BOC e2m system…`, quoted cashier scripts). Each was repaired surgically against the workflow's own intact Pain Points: genuine mitigations kept verbatim (e.g. W1121 → "battery backup and manual megaphone as mandatory backup measures"; W520 → "quarterly spot-audits and verification rate monitoring"), truncated "(e.g., …" tails completed from source (W1330 Yolanda-level typhoon scenarios, W923 partner-probation threshold, W552 prompt-frequency example, W598 deposit/LOI indicators, W1189 90-vs-120-day settings, W543.3 evidence citation), and 15 pure description fragments with no source mitigation dropped (W1233 BOC downtime, W270 RTP informal exchange, W519 AML false-positive/structuring text, W258 ticket hand-off, W319 floating-rate/documentation text, W34 shift-swap/skill-coverage text, W701 system-loss text, W961 cashier script quote, W114 MC No. 4 fragment, W1219 EIS instability/volume text, W1408 emergency-access tail, W1177 phone-number example, W310 barangay example, W1149 billing-complexity fragment, W358 CCTV downtime fragment) — the CTL anchor citation remains on every one of those sections (CTL-XX coverage is unaffected at 100%).
4. **26 mangled bold markers.** 11 Pain-Points bullets closed their `**Label` with a stray `"` instead of `**` (`- **Seasonal capacity shortages": …` → `**Seasonal capacity shortages**:`), 13 already-bulleted Controls lines carried stray `**` artifacts (mostly VS-100.2/100.3 segment tails), and 2 further unbalanced lines were folded into cluster 3. All repaired.
5. **1 Pain-Points bullet written as a table row** (VS-137 PA-137.2 `| **Stale-SDS risk**: …`) — would not render as a bullet or a table; converted to a normal bullet.

**Validator Check 43 added** (analysis-section list hygiene + bold/paren balance: every Controls-section content line must be a bullet or indented continuation; Controls bullets must carry balanced parens and no `**` artifacts; every paragraph block in the model-company docs + root README must have an even `**` count with code spans stripped so glob patterns like `workflows/**/*.md` are exempt). Check-count self-descriptions raised to 43 (root README tree line, 07-methodology README contents row); `fix-controls-bullets.py` registered in both tool listings. **No workflow IDs, titles, tier assignments, register-row counts, CTL mappings, or canonical totals changed** (188 VS · 569 PA · 5,355 WF · 728 Req · 808 CTL · 6,762 HC). After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **43 checks**.

## 2026-08-24 — PDC follow-up: finalize W424 (AP PDC Issuance & Monitoring) Time Estimate from mechanical draft to house-style annualized figure

A follow-up review of the post-dated-check coverage (prompted by "can the system handle PDCs in both collection and disbursements?") confirmed both directions fully modeled — AR side: W1380 (receipt/register/deposit) + W423 (treasury warehousing/clearing) + W1381/W425 (bounced-check resolution); AP side: W424 (issuance/monitoring, with stale-escheatment via W556) — but found W424's Time Estimate still carrying the mechanical per-step roll-up injected by `backfill-time-estimate.py`: the one remaining draft among the PDC workflows (W1380/W1381/W423/W425 already carried authored estimates; the W589/W1202 drafts in PA-18.1 are unrelated to PDC and remain tracked backlog — draft-roll-up count 408 → 407 repo-wide). The section is now authored in the WORKFLOW-FORMAT-GUIDE house style (per-occurrence figure + annualization from the workflow's own Frequency/Volume): ~2.75 hours per lease PDC batch of 12 checks (generation 30 min + DOA signature routing 1 hour + Issued PDC Register recording 15 min + vendor handover 1 hour) × ~17–25 batches/year (200–300 lease PDCs ÷ 12) ≈ 45–70 hours/year, plus ~35 min/month recurring maturity monitoring and clearing reconciliation (~7 hours/year) — total ~55–75 hours/year, absorbed by existing AP Clerk and Treasury Analyst, no incremental headcount. No steps, GL treatment, or cross-references changed. Canonical totals unchanged: **188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,349 workflows · 728 requirements · 808 controls · 6,762 headcount**. After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **41 checks**.

## 2026-08-24 — Post-dated-check (PDC) & bounced-check consistency repair: designate W1380/W1381 the canonical customer-side lifecycles; realign W423/W425 as treasury companions; close 6 AR-side PDC definition gaps; re-point 16 misdirected citations

A domain review of post-dated-check (PDC) handling found the subject covered by **two parallel workflow pairs that contradicted each other**: W423 (AR PDC Warehousing & Clearing, PA-18.1) vs W1380 (Customer PDC Receipt, Register & Deposit, PA-16.3), and W425 (Bounced Check Recovery, PA-18.1) vs W1381 (Bounced Check Resolution, PA-16.3). Canonical totals unchanged: **188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,349 workflows · 728 requirements · 808 controls · 6,762 headcount**. After this pass `validate-repo.sh` reports **0 errors / 0 warnings** across all **39 checks**.

**1. Canonical pair designated and contradictions eliminated.** W1380/W1381 (VS-16) are now the canonical end-to-end customer-side workflows; W423/W425 (VS-18) are rewritten in place as **treasury-side execution companions** with explicit "Canonical workflow" declarations. The repaired contradictions: (a) **GL treatment** — W423 step 3 memo-credited AR with no GL entry until clearing (Dr Cash / Cr AR at step 7) while W1380 posted Dr PDC Receivable / Cr AR at receipt; W423 now defers to W1380's posting and recognizes cash only at deposit/clearing (Dr Cash in Bank / Cr PDC Receivable); (b) **bounce reversal** — W425's single Dr AR / Cr Cash entry vs W1381's mixed entry, both replaced by the explicit two-entry reversal (reverse deposit: Dr PDC Receivable / Cr Cash; reinstate: Dr AR / Cr PDC Receivable); (c) **volumes** — W425 claimed 2–5 bounced checks/month vs W1381's 15–25/month (200–300/year); aligned to W1381; (d) **credit-hold path** — W425 cited W8.1 while W1381/FIN-044 use W572; aligned to W572; (e) **custody & cadence** — dual vault-custodian models and daily-vs-weekly maturity reports unified (weekly 7-day look-ahead for planning + daily T-1 execution list; joint monthly vault count in both files).

**2. Unique content merged so nothing was lost.** W425's penalty charging (Returned Check Fee debit memo, Dr AR / Cr Other Income, PHP 500–2,000 bank charges), DAUD reason code, and double-recovery control ("do not re-present" register flag + single-path redemption confirmation) are merged into W1381 steps 1/2/3 and pain points; W423's vault-custodian handoff, RDC deposit, and clearing confirmation become its companion steps 1–5.

**3. Six AR-side definition gaps closed in W1380/W1381.** (a) **stale-date watch** — 60-day alert on warehoused PDCs approaching the 6-month validity horizon, customer re-issuance demand, GL reversal before expiry (step 8); (b) **customer-requested return / replacement / stop-payment** pre-maturity with GL reversal and acknowledgment receipt (step 9); (c) **on-account & multi-invoice PDC allocation rules** (remittance-advice-then-FIFO; on-account PDCs as customer credit) (step 2d–e); (d) **credit-limit relief rule** — invoices covered by a registered PDC restore available credit, on-account PDCs only upon application, consumed by W572 (step 2f + touchpoint); (e) **monthly physical vault count** reconciled to the PDC register (step 10 / W423 step 7); (f) **cash-forecast feed** — PDC maturities now flow to W233/W30 as scheduled inflows at expected clearing date (previously only AP PDCs fed W233 via W424.5). W424 also gains the stale-dated issued-PDC escheatment cross-reference (W556 / PA-15.2).

**4. Sixteen misdirected citations re-pointed to the canonical workflows.** AR-side references that wrongly pointed at the treasury pair (or at W424): PA-16.1 ×6 NSF-signal refs W425→W1381 (plus 2 new W1380 pointers on PDC-secured credit terms); PA-16.3 ×2 W425→W1381; VS-16 README key-controls W423/W425→W1380/W1381; PA-03.1 ×2 and PA-09.1, PA-15.1, PA-42.2 W423→W1380; PA-43.1 account-closure PDC return W424→W1380; PA-38.2 and PA-11.1 W425→W1381.

**5. Cross-reference registers aligned.** requirement-workflow-matrix FIN-005 supporting list now cites W1380/W1381; FIN-010 cites both the canonical pair and the retitled companions; FIN-044's NSF signal re-pointed W425→W1381. workflow-dependency-map §2.3 B2B chain nodes re-pointed (v4.8); workflow-system-touchpoint-map Financials row gains W1380/W1381 (v76.0); workflow-criticality-classification Core Financial rows W423/W425 retitled to companion scope (v7.31). CTL-288/CTL-293 mappings unchanged (all four workflows remain in their PAs).

## 2026-06-26 — Consistency review #11: 4,618 TOC anchors broken on GitHub (collapsing-slugger bug); fix-toc-anchors.py v2 + Check 23 slugger correction

An independent whole-repo review (validator green at 0 errors / 3 informational warnings across all 25 checks; every headline figure re-derived independently — 188 VS directories / 569 PA files / 5,349 unique `## W` IDs with 0 duplicates / 733 unique requirement IDs with 0 true duplicates after letter-suffix disambiguation / 67 unique controls / root-README folder-tree counts vs disk / index per-VS rows vs disk / all 188 VS-README per-PA count tables & totals vs actual PA headers / all 3,434 relative .md links repo-wide resolving / all 26 non-PA intra-file anchors resolving / 733 matrix rows reconciling) found one systemic defect class invisible to every existing check: **PA TOC anchors that resolve under a collapsing slugger but not on GitHub.** Canonical totals unchanged: **188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,349 workflows · 733 requirements · 67 controls**.

1. **The defect.** GitHub's slugger (github-slugger) removes punctuation and then replaces *each* space with `-` WITHOUT collapsing adjacent hyphens, so spaced punctuation (` & `, ` / `) renders as a **double** hyphen: `W1. Merchandise Planning & Assortment Review` → `#w1-merchandise-planning--assortment-review`. The PA TOC generator (and, inadvertently, review #9's repairer and validator Check 23) collapsed `[\s_-]+` runs to one `-`, producing `#w1-merchandise-planning-assortment-review` — dead links on GitHub for every heading containing `&`, `/`, or similar spaced punctuation. A scan with a GitHub-accurate slugger flagged **4,618 such anchors across 554 PA files** (the whole gap-analysis block plus Expansion-block files; Core-block VS-01–VS-31 headings mostly lack `&`, so few were hit). Review #9's 1,926-anchor fix was real but incomplete: it repaired anchors that failed even the collapsing rule, while wrongly-collapsed ones kept passing Check 23 because the check shared the bug.
2. **`fix-toc-anchors.py` v2** — slugger corrected to github-slugger semantics (no run-collapsing; duplicate headings get GitHub's `-N` suffixes); docstring documents the defect class. Re-run repaired **4,618 anchors across 554 PA files, 0 skipped** (3 display-text typos resolved via the W-number fallback rule). Verified a **pure anchor swap**: every ± diff line identical modulo the `#w…` anchor, display text byte-identical.
3. **Validator Check 23 slugger corrected** to the same GitHub-accurate semantics (with duplicate-suffix handling), so the false-green class cannot recur. Still 25 checks; still 0 errors / 3 informational warnings post-fix, and an independent from-scratch checker confirms 0 unresolved intra-file anchors repo-wide.
4. Also verified clean this pass (no action needed): root-README tree counts, VS-README per-PA tables, index per-VS rows, cross-file fragment links (4/4 resolve), REQ/CTL/workflow-ID uniqueness, and no stale canonical figures (5,317 / 5,341 appear only inside versioned historical change-notes).

## 2026-06-26 — Fix 1,926 broken intra-file TOC anchors; add validator Check 23

Every PA file opens with a `## Workflows in This Process Area` navigation list whose entries link to the workflow headings below via GitHub heading anchors (`#slug`). A review using GitHub's exact slugger found **1,926 of these anchors malformed across 271 PA files** — every one in the gap-analysis block (VS-89+), zero in Core (VS-01–VS-31). Two malformation patterns from one generator bug: (a) the slugger dropped hyphens (`Floor-Plan` → `floorplan`; `stocking-list` → `stockinglist`); (b) a stale W-number survived a renumber (display text `W4601` but anchor `#w4409-…`). The TOC display text was always correct, so clicking the link did nothing on GitHub. No prior check saw this — Checks 19/20 verify linked *files* resolve; none resolved anchors.

1. **`fix-toc-anchors.py` (new)** — regenerates each broken anchor from its (correct) display text using GitHub's slug rules. Safe rule: for every `(#anchor)` that doesn't resolve, use `gh_slug(display)` if it matches a heading (1,923 cases), else fall back to the unique heading sharing the display's W-number (3 truncated/typo'd display-text cases). Idempotent (a resolved anchor is never rewritten). The fix is a pure in-place anchor swap — **+1,926 / −1,926 diff lines, 0 non-anchor lines changed**, display text byte-identical.
2. **Validator Check 23 (new)** — intra-file TOC anchor resolution. Computes each file's heading slugs and ERRORs on any `(#anchor)` matching no heading (an ERROR, consistent with the file-resolution checks 19/20, since a broken anchor is a broken navigational link). Verified to catch a deliberately-injected broken anchor.

`validate-repo.sh`: **0 errors / 3 informational warnings** across all **23** checks. Canonical totals unchanged: 188 value streams · 569 process areas · 5,349 workflows · 733 requirements · 67 controls.

## 2026-06-21 — Pass 30: One new value stream (VS-192; W5465–W5488; +24 workflows)

**VS-192 Green Fleet Transition, Electric Vehicle (EV) Fleet Operations & Sustainable Transportation** (Make & Move). A genuinely-uncovered emerging discipline: the defining terms 'fleet electrification', 'electric fleet', 'EV fleet', 'green fleet', and 'fleet decarbonization' each appeared in **zero** PA files as dedicated workflow headers, and the capability was in fact self-acknowledged as a forward-referenced need inside VS-163 PA-163.1 step 1 (*"BuildRight's own EV-fleet charging need (VS-06/VS-61 green-fleet transition)") without an owning value stream. Driven by RA 11697 (EVIDA, 2022) — the Philippine EV Industry Development Act whose IRR includes commercial/fleet adoption — and by BuildRight's stated ESG decarbonization posture (VS-25), rooftop-solar prosumer program (VS-108), and existing EV-charging host network (VS-163). The value stream owns the multi-year decarbonization of BuildRight's own goods-moving and service fleet (the ~20% owned share of the outbound-distribution fleet VS-06, plus the rental fleets VS-162/VS-186, jobsite-delivery VS-74, and employee shuttles VS-141) and the charging/alt-fuel infrastructure, route-network redesign, and EVIDA/DOE/LTO compliance the transition requires. Distinct from VS-06 (operates the current diesel fleet — this transitions it), VS-61 (diesel fuel/cost — this owns electricity/alt-fuel energy), VS-163 (hosts customer EV charging — this owns charging for BuildRight's own fleet), VS-108 (own-generation solar — this matches fleet charging load to it), and VS-25 (reports the footprint — this reduces it). (See Gap Analysis table below.)

**Reconciled counts** across `value-stream-index.md`, both `README.md` files, `WORKFLOW-FORMAT-GUIDE.md`, `headcount-reality-check.md`, `requirement-workflow-matrix.md`, `workflow-dependency-map.md`, `workflow-system-touchpoint-map.md`, and `workflow-criticality-classification.md`: **188 value streams · 568 process areas · 5,341 workflows** (Make & Move 475→499); unclassified **2,564 → 2,588**, all carrying a proposed tier (`workflow-criticality-proposed.md` regenerated via `07-methodology/classify-workflows.py`). ## 2026-06-21 — Required-field completeness: validator Check 22, backfill 410 Time Estimates, fix W2796 Volume

A scan against the 9 required fields in `WORKFLOW-FORMAT-GUIDE.md` found **1,105 missing-field instances** across ~1,000 workflows — a generation artifact where whole VS batches (VS-27/37/38/39/40/53/56/58/60/63) shipped missing analysis sections, undetected because no prior check enforced required-field completeness (Check 12 covered only Automation/Controls). All structural/mechanically-derivable gaps are closed below; the content-field remainder is tracked as the honest backlog. `validate-repo.sh` now reports **0 errors / 3 informational warnings** across all **22** checks.

**Canonical totals (unchanged):** 187 value streams · 565 process areas · 5,317 workflows · 733 requirements · 67 controls · W1–W5464.

1. **Validator Check 22 (new)** — required-field completeness. Reports missing instances per field as a WARN. Detector accepts all legitimate forms: parenthetically-qualified section headers (`### Steps (...)`, `### System Touchpoints (Yard)`), phase/cadence-grouped step tables (`### Daily`/`### Weekly`), non-canonical columns, and alpha-prefixed step IDs (`CS-1`/`NR-1`) — verified that 16 initial "missing Steps" flags were all false positives on richly-structured workflows. Final reading: **645 missing** (all content fields — Participants 224, System Touchpoints 175, Pain Points 246; structural fields all 0).
2. **`backfill-time-estimate.py` (new)** — mechanically derived **410 `### Time Estimate` sections** from each workflow's own per-step Durations, explicitly labelled a draft pending human annualization. Honest-draft principle: values come from already-authored step Durations; no fabricated throughput math (receipts/year etc. is domain content). Insertion lands inside the workflow body before the `---` separator, so Check 16 (PA-footer) is unaffected. Idempotent. Time Estimate gap: 410 → **0**.
3. **Fixed 1 genuine structural defect** — `W2796` (VS-80 PA-80.3) was missing its `| **Volume** |` row (a dropped table row); restored from the Frequency field's "~600 terminals". Volume gap: 1 → **0**.
4. **Deferred (content fields, require human authoring):** Participants (224), System Touchpoints (175), Pain Points / Risks (246) — **645 instances** across ~1,000 workflows in VS-27/37/38/39/40/53/56/58/60/63. These are the format guide's workflow-specific content fields ("name the specific module/object", "named specifically, with the mitigating control"); bulk-generating them would repeat the draft-content-claimed-as-complete mistake the prior reviews corrected. Check 22 now tracks this backlog measurably.
